## Task 5: 判题链路端到端 —— 五类 verdict + 四型断言全覆盖

**Files:**
- Create: `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-judge-e2e.test.js`
- Modify（**仅在测试暴露问题时才动**）: `projects/apps/study-hub/judge/runner.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `judge/runner.js` 的 `judgeUnit(params) → Promise<UnitJudgeResult>`（Task 4 落的）
  - `judge/asserts.js` 的 `validateAsserts(asserts, run)`（Task 2 落的）
  - `judge/sandbox.js` 的 `detectPython()`（本文件用它判断本机能不能跑 Python）；
    `createSandbox()` / `copyIntoSandbox()` / `copyDirIntoSandbox()` / `collectArtifacts()`
    由 `judgeUnit` 间接消费（Task 3 落的）
  - P0 已落的 `learning/units/database/{mvcc,transaction,index_design,sql_fundamentals}.assert.py`
    与 `learning/scaffold/grokking/<stem>/main.py`（**只读**，一个字节都不改）
- Produces: 一条把「断言集 → 选入口 → 沙箱执行 → 收集产物 → 逐条校验 → verdict」整条链路钉死的端到端测试

> **这个 Task 不写 learning 仓。** P0 的 Task 10 已经落了那 4 个 `.assert.py`，并跑过实跑 + 变异测试；
> P2 的 Task 5 **只读引用**它们——把它们接进 `judgeUnit` 跑一遍，确认 verdict 是 `pass`。
> 所以本 Task **不查 learning 仓的脏、不新建 learning 仓的文件、不提交 learning 仓**。

**覆盖矩阵：**

| 维度 | 场景 | 期望 verdict |
|---|---|---|
| verdict | 断言全过 | `pass` |
| verdict | 输出与期望不匹配 | `wrong_answer` |
| verdict | 脚本挂住不退出 | `timeout` |
| verdict | 断言脚本不存在 | `compile_error`（带中文说明，**不是 500、不是抛异常**） |
| verdict | 脚本自己抛异常 | `runtime_error` |
| 断言类型 | `output` | 通过 / 不通过各一条 |
| 断言类型 | `exit` | 通过（`expect: 0`）/ 不通过（`expect` 非法）各一条 |
| 断言类型 | `contains` | 通过 / 不通过各一条 |
| 断言类型 | `file` | 通过 / 不通过各一条 |
| 污染 | 判完之后 learning 仓 | 文件清单前后完全一致 |
| 真实资产 | P0 的 4 个数据库卡脚本 | 4 条全 `pass` |

**五类 verdict 一类都不能少**：契约 §12 明写「五类 verdict 全部可达」，少一类就是 P2 没做完。
`compile_error` 这一条尤其重要——契约 §12 的 P2 验收线明文要求「断言脚本缺失 → `compile_error` + 中文说明，
**不是 500、不是抛异常**」。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试文件**

Create `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-judge-e2e.test.js`：

```javascript
"use strict"
/**
 * p2-judge-e2e.test.js —— 判题链路的端到端验收
 *
 * 覆盖：
 *   1. 五类 verdict 各一条：pass / wrong_answer / timeout / compile_error / runtime_error
 *   2. 四型断言各一条：output / exit / contains / file
 *   3. 零污染：判完之后 learning 仓不多出任何文件
 *   4. 真实资产：P0 已落的 4 个数据库卡断言脚本经 judgeUnit → pass
 *
 * 本文件**只读**引用 learning 仓（P0 的 Task 10 已经落了那 4 个断言脚本），
 * 自己不写 learning 仓、不提交 learning 仓。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert/strict")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")

const runner = require("../judge/runner.js")
const sandboxMod = require("../judge/sandbox.js")
const assertsMod = require("../judge/asserts.js")

const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..")

/**
 * 定位 learning 仓：先看环境变量，再看仓库默认布局（apps/study-hub 上三级）。
 * 两处都找不到就跳过——但**必须给出理由**，不许静默跳过。
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
function resolveLearningRoot() {
  const fromEnv = process.env.STUDY_HUB_LEARNING_ROOT
  if (fromEnv && fs.existsSync(fromEnv)) return path.resolve(fromEnv)
  const fallback = path.resolve(ROOT, "..", "..", "..", "learning")
  if (fs.existsSync(fallback)) return fallback
  return null
}

const LEARNING_ROOT = resolveLearningRoot()
const PYTHON = sandboxMod.detectPython()

/**
 * 造一个临时 learning 仓。传入「相对路径 → 文件内容」的映射。
 * @param {Object<string,string>} files
 * @returns {string} 临时仓根目录
 */
function makeLearningRoot(files) {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "study-hub-e2e-"))
  Object.keys(files || {}).forEach(function (rel) {
    const abs = path.join(root, rel)
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true })
    fs.writeFileSync(abs, files[rel], "utf8")
  })
  return root
}

/**
 * 列出一个目录下的全部文件（相对路径，斜杠分隔）。`.git` 跳过。
 * 用它来证明「判完之后 learning 仓没有多出任何文件」。
 * @param {string} root
 * @returns {Array<string>}
 */
function listFiles(root) {
  const out = []
  const walk = function (dir) {
    let names = []
    try {
      names = fs.readdirSync(dir)
    } catch (e) {
      return
    }
    names.sort().forEach(function (name) {
      if (name === ".git") return
      const abs = path.join(dir, name)
      let st = null
      try {
        st = fs.statSync(abs)
      } catch (e) {
        return
      }
      if (st.isDirectory()) walk(abs)
      else out.push(path.relative(root, abs).split(path.sep).join("/"))
    })
  }
  walk(root)
  return out
}

/**
 * 按契约 §3.5 的模板生成一个断言脚本。
 * 骨架与冻结模板一致：subprocess 跑 demo（不 import）、把 demo 复制进临时目录再跑、
 * 抓字节按 utf-8 → gbk 依次试解码（**不写死 text=True**）、
 * 全过打印「全部断言通过（N 条）」、有失败打印「断言失败：…」并 exit 1、
 * 失败时把 demo 的 stderr 转发到脚本自己的 stderr。
 * @param {{checks?: Array<[string,string]>, pre?: string}} opts
 * @returns {string} Python 源码
 */
function makeScript(opts) {
  const o = opts || {}
  const checks = o.checks || []
  const L = []
  L.push("import os")
  L.push("import shutil")
  L.push("import subprocess")
  L.push("import sys")
  L.push("import tempfile")
  L.push("")
  L.push("DEMO = os.environ.get(\"STUDY_HUB_DEMO\") or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), \"main.py\")")
  L.push("CHECKS = [")
  checks.forEach(function (c) {
    L.push("    (" + JSON.stringify(c[0]) + ", " + JSON.stringify(c[1]) + "),")
  })
  L.push("]")
  L.push("")
  L.push("")
  L.push("def decode(raw):")
  L.push("    for enc in (\"utf-8\", \"gbk\"):")
  L.push("        try:")
  L.push("            return raw.decode(enc)")
  L.push("        except UnicodeDecodeError:")
  L.push("            continue")
  L.push("    return raw.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")")
  L.push("")
  L.push("")
  L.push("def run_demo():")
  L.push("    sandbox = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"study-hub-assert-\")")
  L.push("    try:")
  L.push("        target = os.path.join(sandbox, os.path.basename(DEMO))")
  L.push("        shutil.copy2(DEMO, target)")
  L.push("        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, target], capture_output=True, timeout=180)")
  L.push("        return proc, None")
  L.push("    finally:")
  L.push("        shutil.rmtree(sandbox, ignore_errors=True)")
  L.push("")
  L.push("")
  L.push("def main():")
  if (o.pre) {
    o.pre.split("\n").forEach(function (line) {
      L.push("    " + line)
    })
  }
  L.push("    proc, err = run_demo()")
  L.push("    if err is not None:")
  L.push("        print(err)")
  L.push("        return 1")
  L.push("    out = decode(proc.stdout)")
  L.push("    print(\"─── demo 输出开始 ───\")")
  L.push("    print(out.rstrip())")
  L.push("    print(\"─── demo 输出结束 ───\")")
  L.push("    failed = []")
  L.push("    for name, needle in CHECKS:")
  L.push("        if needle not in out:")
  L.push("            failed.append(name + \"（缺少：\" + needle + \"）\")")
  L.push("    if proc.returncode != 0:")
  L.push("        failed.append(\"demo 退出码非 0：\" + str(proc.returncode))")
  L.push("        failed.append(\"demo 的 stderr 末尾：\" + decode(proc.stderr)[-500:])")
  L.push("    if failed:")
  L.push("        for item in failed:")
  L.push("            print(\"断言失败：\" + item)")
  L.push("        print(\"─── demo 错误输出开始 ───\", file=sys.stderr)")
  L.push("        print(decode(proc.stderr).rstrip(), file=sys.stderr)")
  L.push("        print(\"─── demo 错误输出结束 ───\", file=sys.stderr)")
  L.push("        return 1")
  L.push("    print(\"全部断言通过（%d 条）\" % len(CHECKS))")
  L.push("    return 0")
  L.push("")
  L.push("")
  L.push("if __name__ == \"__main__\":")
  L.push("    sys.exit(main())")
  L.push("")
  return L.join("\n")
}

/**
 * 造一张卡类单元（graded = script）。
 * @param {string} id
 * @param {string} scriptRel 相对 learning 仓根的断言脚本路径
 * @param {string} demoRel 相对 learning 仓根的 demo 路径
 * @returns {object}
 */
function cardUnit(id, scriptRel, demoRel) {
  return {
    id: id,
    track: "database",
    kind: "card",
    title: "端到端测试卡",
    entry: { demo: demoRel, reference: null, graded: "script", script: scriptRel, language: "python" },
    asserts: [],
    path: scriptRel,
  }
}

/**
 * 造一道题目类单元（graded = user）。
 * @param {string} id
 * @param {Array<object>} asserts
 * @returns {object}
 */
function problemUnit(id, asserts) {
  return {
    id: id,
    track: "algorithm",
    kind: "problem",
    title: "端到端测试题",
    entry: { demo: null, reference: null, graded: "user", script: null, language: "python" },
    asserts: asserts,
    path: "bank/algorithm/" + id + ".md",
  }
}

const DEMO_OK = ["print(\"hello demo\")", ""].join("\n")

/** 本机没有 Python 时统一用的跳过理由。 */
function needPython(t) {
  t.skip("本机没有 Python 解释器（python3 / python / py -3 都试过了），判不了")
  return false
}
```

接着往同一个文件里追加测试本体（**仍然是 `tests/p2-judge-e2e.test.js`**，接着上面那段追加）：

```javascript
// ── 五类 verdict ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

test("verdict · pass：断言全过", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const root = makeLearningRoot({
    "demo/main.py": DEMO_OK,
    "units/database/ok.assert.py": makeScript({ checks: [["演示输出", "hello demo"]] }),
  })
  try {
    const unit = cardUnit("db:e2e-ok", "units/database/ok.assert.py", "demo/main.py")
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: root })
    assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "pass", r.message)
    assert.ok(r.message.indexOf("通过") !== -1, r.message)
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

test("verdict · wrong_answer：输出与期望不匹配", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const root = makeLearningRoot({
    "demo/main.py": DEMO_OK,
    "units/database/no.assert.py": makeScript({ checks: [["不存在的结论", "这句话不在 demo 输出里"]] }),
  })
  try {
    const unit = cardUnit("db:e2e-no", "units/database/no.assert.py", "demo/main.py")
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: root })
    assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "wrong_answer", r.message)
    assert.ok(r.stdout.indexOf("断言失败") !== -1, "脚本应当打印中文失败原因：" + r.stdout)
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

test("verdict · timeout：脚本挂住不退出", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const root = makeLearningRoot({
    "demo/main.py": DEMO_OK,
    "units/database/hang.assert.py": makeScript({ pre: "import time\ntime.sleep(999)" }),
  })
  try {
    const unit = cardUnit("db:e2e-hang", "units/database/hang.assert.py", "demo/main.py")
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: root, timeoutMs: 1500 })
    assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "timeout", r.message)
    assert.ok(r.message.indexOf("超时") !== -1, r.message)
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

test("verdict · compile_error：断言脚本不存在（中文说明，不是 500、不是抛异常）", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const root = makeLearningRoot({ "demo/main.py": DEMO_OK })
  try {
    const unit = cardUnit("db:e2e-missing", "units/database/missing.assert.py", "demo/main.py")
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: root })
    assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "compile_error", r.message)
    assert.ok(r.message.indexOf("断言脚本不存在") !== -1, r.message)
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

test("verdict · runtime_error：断言脚本自己抛异常", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const root = makeLearningRoot({
    "demo/main.py": DEMO_OK,
    "units/database/boom.assert.py": makeScript({ pre: "raise RuntimeError(\"boom\")" }),
  })
  try {
    const unit = cardUnit("db:e2e-boom", "units/database/boom.assert.py", "demo/main.py")
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: root })
    assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "runtime_error", r.message)
    assert.ok(r.message.indexOf("崩了") !== -1, r.message)
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

// ── 四型断言 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

test("断言 · output：匹配得上 → pass", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-output-ok", [{ type: "output", input: "1 2", expect: "3", cmp: "exact" }])
  const code = "a, b = map(int, input().split())\nprint(a + b)\n"
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: code, language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "pass", r.message)
  assert.strictEqual(r.passedCases, 1)
})

test("断言 · output：匹配不上 → wrong_answer，failedCase.type 是 output", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-output-no", [{ type: "output", input: "1 2", expect: "3", cmp: "exact" }])
  const code = "a, b = map(int, input().split())\nprint(a * b)\n"
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: code, language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "wrong_answer", r.message)
  assert.ok(r.failedCase, "必须指出是哪一条断言没过")
  assert.strictEqual(r.failedCase.type, "output")
})

test("断言 · exit：expect 0 → pass", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-exit-ok", [{ type: "exit", expect: 0 }])
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: "print('ok')\n", language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "pass", r.message)
})

test("断言 · exit：expect 非法 → wrong_answer，理由是中文", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-exit-bad", [{ type: "exit", expect: 1 }])
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: "print('ok')\n", language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "wrong_answer", r.message)
  assert.ok(r.failedCase, "必须指出是哪一条断言没过")
  assert.strictEqual(r.failedCase.type, "exit")
  assert.ok(r.failedCase.reason.indexOf("断言非法") !== -1, r.failedCase.reason)
})

test("断言 · contains：找得到 → pass", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-contains-ok", [{ type: "contains", text: "答案" }])
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: "print('答案是 42')\n", language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "pass", r.message)
})

test("断言 · contains：找不到 → wrong_answer，failedCase.type 是 contains", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-contains-no", [{ type: "contains", text: "这个片段不存在" }])
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: "print('答案是 42')\n", language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "wrong_answer", r.message)
  assert.ok(r.failedCase, "必须指出是哪一条断言没过")
  assert.strictEqual(r.failedCase.type, "contains")
})

test("断言 · file：产物对得上 → pass", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-file-ok", [{ type: "file", path: "out.txt", expect: "hello" }])
  // 写文件必须显式 encoding="utf-8"：本机默认编码是 GBK，
  // 而 readArtifact 读的是 utf-8（契约 §2 的 file 口径）。
  const code = "open('out.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('hello')\n"
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: code, language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "pass", r.message)
})

test("断言 · file：产物对不上 → wrong_answer，failedCase.type 是 file", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  const unit = problemUnit("algo:e2e-file-no", [{ type: "file", path: "out.txt", expect: "hello" }])
  const code = "open('out.txt', 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('world')\n"
  const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, code: code, language: "python" })
  assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "wrong_answer", r.message)
  assert.ok(r.failedCase, "必须指出是哪一条断言没过")
  assert.strictEqual(r.failedCase.type, "file")
})

test("四型断言的校验器本身也认这四种类型", function () {
  const res = assertsMod.validateAsserts(
    [
      { type: "output", expect: "hi", cmp: "exact" },
      { type: "exit", expect: 0 },
      { type: "contains", text: "hi" },
      { type: "file", path: "out.txt", expect: "hi" },
    ],
    { stdout: "hi", stderr: "", exitCode: 0, timedOut: false, artifacts: { "out.txt": "hi" } }
  )
  assert.strictEqual(res.total, 4)
  assert.strictEqual(res.passed, 4)
  assert.deepStrictEqual(res.failures, [])
})

// ── 零污染 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

test("零污染：判完之后 learning 仓不多出任何文件", async function (t) {
  if (!PYTHON) {
    needPython(t)
    return
  }
  // demo 会在自己旁边写 .db——正是要盯住的那种污染
  const demoWithDb = [
    "import os",
    "open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'demo.db'), 'w').write('x')",
    "print('hello demo')",
    "",
  ].join("\n")
  const root = makeLearningRoot({
    "demo/main.py": demoWithDb,
    "units/database/ok.assert.py": makeScript({ checks: [["演示输出", "hello demo"]] }),
  })
  try {
    const before = listFiles(root)
    const unit = cardUnit("db:e2e-clean", "units/database/ok.assert.py", "demo/main.py")
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: root })
    const after = listFiles(root)
    assert.strictEqual(r.verdict, "pass", r.message)
    assert.deepStrictEqual(after, before, "learning 仓里不能多出任何文件")
    assert.strictEqual(after.filter(function (f) { return /\.db$/.test(f) }).length, 0)
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true })
  }
})

// ── 真实资产：P0 已落的 4 个数据库卡 ─────────────────────────────

const DB_CARDS = ["mvcc", "transaction", "index_design", "sql_fundamentals"]

DB_CARDS.forEach(function (stem) {
  test("真实资产 · " + stem + " 的断言脚本经 judgeUnit → pass", async function (t) {
    if (!LEARNING_ROOT) {
      t.skip("找不到 learning 仓：设 STUDY_HUB_LEARNING_ROOT 或在仓库默认布局下运行")
      return
    }
    if (!PYTHON) {
      needPython(t)
      return
    }
    const scriptRel = "units/database/" + stem + ".assert.py"
    const demoRel = "scaffold/grokking/" + stem + "/main.py"
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(LEARNING_ROOT, scriptRel)), "断言脚本不存在：" + scriptRel)
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(LEARNING_ROOT, demoRel)), "demo 不存在：" + demoRel)

    const unit = cardUnit("db:" + stem, scriptRel, demoRel)
    const r = await runner.judgeUnit({ unit: unit, learningRoot: LEARNING_ROOT })
    assert.strictEqual(
      r.verdict,
      "pass",
      stem + " 判题没通过：" + r.message + "\nstdout:\n" + r.stdout + "\nstderr:\n" + r.stderr
    )
    assert.ok(r.stdout.indexOf("全部断言通过") !== -1, "脚本应当打印通过标记：" + r.stdout)
  })
})
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `projects/apps/study-hub/judge/sandbox.js`（追加 `detectNumpy`）
- Modify: `projects/apps/study-hub/core/track/tracks.js`（追加 `GROKKING_CARDS` / `NUMPY_CARDS` / `cardNeedsNumpy`）

- Create: `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-loader-b.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 契约 §3.2（loader B 的 `parseCardDirectory`）、§14.1（16 + 13 的冻结清单与 numpy 依赖）、Task 1 Step 8 增补的四处形状
- Produces:
  - `judge/sandbox.js` → `detectNumpy(pythonOverride?) : { available: boolean, version: string }`
  - `core/track/tracks.js` → `GROKKING_CARDS : {database: string[], ai: string[]}`、`NUMPY_CARDS : string[]`、`cardNeedsNumpy(stem) : boolean`
  - `core/unit/loader-b.js` → `parseCardDirectory` 多认一个选填的 `opts.env.numpy`

**磁盘现状（已核对，2026-11-13）：** `learning/scaffold/grokking/` 共 **88** 个目录，其中 **59** 个带 `main.py`（且这 59 个都有 `README.md` 与 `NOTES.md`）；这 59 张里属于本轮三轨的正好 **29** 张（db 16 + ai 13），另外 30 张是纯算法 / C++ 并发卡，不在本轮范围。`import numpy` 的卡正好 **14** 张 = `clustering` + 13 张 AI 卡——与契约 §14.1 完全对得上。

**P2 在这一层要落的三件事：**
1. 把「哪 29 张属于三轨」「哪 14 张需要 numpy」**写成代码里的数据表**（契约 §14.1 是清单，代码要能查）。
2. **缺 numpy 时要说清楚**：AI 卡的 demo 依赖 numpy，本机没装就跑不了——判题时给「环境缺失」的中文说明，单元页上也要标出来，不许让用户对着一个 `ModuleNotFoundError` 发懵。
3. **loader B 读出来的每一张卡都要能直接用**（id / kind / status / entry / 讲解正文全对）。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

Create `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-loader-b.test.js`：

```javascript
"use strict"
/**
 * p2-loader-b.test.js —— loader B 的 DB/AI 卡接入
 *
 * 三件事：
 *   1. 契约 §14.1 冻结的 16 张数据库卡 + 13 张 AI 卡，磁盘上一张不少；
 *   2. numpy 依赖表跟磁盘上的真实 import 对得上；
 *   3. 每一张卡都能被 loader B 读成能直接用的单元。
 *
 * AI 卡依赖 numpy；本机没装 numpy 时**明确 skip 并写明原因**，不静默跳过。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")
const { spawnSync } = require("node:child_process")

const tracks = require("../core/track/tracks.js")
const loaderB = require("../core/unit/loader-b.js")
const { detectPython, detectNumpy } = require("../judge/sandbox.js")

/** 契约 §14.1 冻结的数据库轨 16 张 */
const DB_CARDS = [
  "anti_patterns", "backup_recovery", "cache_strategy", "cap_theorem",
  "clustering", "index_design", "lock_manager", "mvcc", "newsql",
  "normalization", "nosql", "query_optimization", "replication",
  "sharding", "sql_fundamentals", "transaction",
]

/** 契约 §14.1 冻结的 AI 轨 13 张 */
const AI_CARDS = [
  "cnn", "decision_tree", "dimensionality_reduction", "gradient_boosting",
  "linear_regression", "logistic_regression", "ml_fundamentals",
  "neural_network", "nlp", "random_forest", "recommendation", "rnn", "svm",
]

function resolveLearningRoot() {
  const fromEnv = process.env.STUDY_HUB_LEARNING_ROOT
  if (fromEnv && fs.existsSync(fromEnv)) return fromEnv
  const guess = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", "..", "..", "learning")
  if (fs.existsSync(guess)) return guess
  return null
}

function grokkingDir(learningRoot) {
  return path.join(learningRoot, "scaffold", "grokking")
}

function skipNoLearning(t, what) {
  t.skip("找不到 learning 仓（设 STUDY_HUB_LEARNING_ROOT，或确认 ../../../../learning 存在），跳过" + what)
}

test("契约 §14.1 的两张清单与 tracks.js 的表一致", function () {
  assert.deepStrictEqual(tracks.GROKKING_CARDS.database.slice().sort(), DB_CARDS.slice().sort())
  assert.deepStrictEqual(tracks.GROKKING_CARDS.ai.slice().sort(), AI_CARDS.slice().sort())
  assert.strictEqual(DB_CARDS.length, 16)
  assert.strictEqual(AI_CARDS.length, 13)
})

test("两张清单没有交集", function () {
  const dup = DB_CARDS.filter(function (s) { return AI_CARDS.indexOf(s) !== -1 })
  assert.deepStrictEqual(dup, [])
})

test("cardNeedsNumpy：clustering 与 13 张 AI 卡为真，其余为假", function () {
  assert.strictEqual(tracks.cardNeedsNumpy("clustering"), true)
  AI_CARDS.forEach(function (s) {
    assert.strictEqual(tracks.cardNeedsNumpy(s), true, s + " 应该需要 numpy")
  })
  DB_CARDS.filter(function (s) { return s !== "clustering" }).forEach(function (s) {
    assert.strictEqual(tracks.cardNeedsNumpy(s), false, s + " 不该需要 numpy")
  })
})

test("numpy 依赖表与磁盘上的真实 import 一致", function (t) {
  const learningRoot = resolveLearningRoot()
  if (!learningRoot) {
    skipNoLearning(t, "numpy 依赖表核对")
    return
  }
  const dir = grokkingDir(learningRoot)
  const stems = fs.readdirSync(dir).filter(function (name) {
    return fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, name, "main.py"))
  })
  stems.forEach(function (stem) {
    const src = fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, stem, "main.py"), "utf8")
    const usesNumpy = src.indexOf("import numpy") !== -1
    assert.strictEqual(
      tracks.cardNeedsNumpy(stem),
      usesNumpy,
      stem + " 的 numpy 依赖与表里对不上"
    )
  })
})

test("29 张卡在磁盘上一张不少", function (t) {
  const learningRoot = resolveLearningRoot()
  if (!learningRoot) {
    skipNoLearning(t, "卡片清点")
    return
  }
  const dir = grokkingDir(learningRoot)
  DB_CARDS.concat(AI_CARDS).forEach(function (stem) {
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, stem, "main.py")), stem + " 缺 main.py")
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, stem, "README.md")), stem + " 缺 README.md")
  })
})

test("loader B 把 16 张数据库卡都读成能用的单元", function (t) {
  const learningRoot = resolveLearningRoot()
  if (!learningRoot) {
    skipNoLearning(t, "loader B 解析验收")
    return
  }

  DB_CARDS.forEach(function (stem) {

      learningRoot: learningRoot,

    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.entry.language, "python")
    assert.deepStrictEqual(out.unit.asserts, [])
    assert.ok(out.unit.statement.length > 0, stem + " 的讲解正文是空的")
  })
})

test("loader B 把 13 张 AI 卡都读成能用的单元", function (t) {
  const learningRoot = resolveLearningRoot()
  if (!learningRoot) {
    skipNoLearning(t, "loader B 解析验收")
    return
  }
  const dir = grokkingDir(learningRoot)
  const numpy = detectNumpy(null)
  AI_CARDS.forEach(function (stem) {
    const out = loaderB.parseCardDirectory(path.join(dir, stem), {
      learningRoot: learningRoot,
      track: "ai",
      unitsDir: path.join(learningRoot, "units"),

    })
    assert.ok(out.unit, stem + " 解析不出单元：" + JSON.stringify(out.warnings))
    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.id, "ai:" + stem)
    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.track, "ai")
    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.kind, "card")
    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.entry.graded, "script")
    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.entry.demo, "scaffold/grokking/" + stem + "/main.py")
    assert.strictEqual(out.unit.entry.script, "units/ai/" + stem + ".assert.py")
    assert.ok(out.unit.statement.length > 0, stem + " 的讲解正文是空的")
  })
})

test("缺 numpy 时 AI 卡的 warnings 里写明原因", function (t) {
  const learningRoot = resolveLearningRoot()
  if (!learningRoot) {
    skipNoLearning(t, "numpy 降级验收")
    return
  }

  assert.ok(out.warnings.join("；").indexOf("numpy") !== -1, "warnings 里要写明缺 numpy")
})

test("detectNumpy：结果可用且能清缓存", function () {
  const numpy = detectNumpy(null)
  assert.strictEqual(typeof numpy.available, "boolean")
  assert.strictEqual(typeof numpy.version, "string")
  if (numpy.available) {
    assert.ok(numpy.version.length > 0, "有 numpy 时要给出版本号")
  }
})

test("AI 卡的 demo 实跑：没有 numpy 时明确 skip 并写明原因", function (t) {
  const learningRoot = resolveLearningRoot()
  if (!learningRoot) {
    skipNoLearning(t, "AI 卡实跑验收")
    return
  }

    const r = spawnSync(py.cmd, py.args.concat([path.join(tmp, stem, "main.py")]), {

  }
})

- [ ] **Step 3: 写实现**

**3.1 `judge/sandbox.js` —— 加 `detectNumpy`**

**3.1.1** 把探测缓存那一行改成：

```javascript
/** 探测结果缓存，避免每次判题都 spawn 一遍探测 */
const detectionCache = { compiler: undefined, python: undefined, numpy: undefined }
```

**3.1.2** 把 `resetDetectionCache` 改成：

```javascript
/** 清空探测缓存（测试用） */
function resetDetectionCache() {
  detectionCache.compiler = undefined
  detectionCache.python = undefined
  detectionCache.numpy = undefined
}
```

**3.1.3** 在 `function resolveToolchain(options) {` **之前**插入：

```javascript
/**
 * 探测 numpy 是否可用。
 * AI 轨的 13 张卡与 clustering 卡都 import numpy，缺了它们整张卡跑不了。
 * 缺 numpy 不是错误状态，只是「这张卡暂时跑不了」——由调用方决定怎么降级。
 * @param {string|null} [override] 指定解释器，形如 "python" 或 "py -3"
 * @returns {{available: boolean, version: string}}
 */
function detectNumpy(override) {
  if (detectionCache.numpy !== undefined) return detectionCache.numpy
  const python = detectPython(override || null)
  if (!python) {
    detectionCache.numpy = { available: false, version: "" }
    return detectionCache.numpy
  }
  const run = spawnSync(
    python.cmd,
    python.args.concat(["-c", "import numpy; print(numpy.__version__)"]),
    {
      timeout: DETECT_TIMEOUT_MS,
      encoding: "utf8",
      windowsHide: true,
    }
  )
  const stdout = String((run && run.stdout) || "")
  if (run && !run.error && run.status === 0 && stdout.trim() !== "") {
    detectionCache.numpy = {
      available: true,
      version: stdout.trim().split("\n")[0].trim(),
    }
  } else {
    detectionCache.numpy = { available: false, version: "" }
  }
  return detectionCache.numpy
}
```

**3.1.4** 在 `module.exports` 里加一行 `detectNumpy: detectNumpy,`（紧跟 `detectPython: detectPython,` 后面）。

**3.2 `core/track/tracks.js` —— 加两张表与一个判定**

在 `module.exports` **之前**追加（既有导出一个字都不动）：

```javascript
/**
 * grokking 卡的清单（接口契约 §14.1 冻结）。
 * database 16 张 + ai 13 张 = 29 张，是本轮三轨的卡片范围。
 */
const GROKKING_CARDS = {
  database: [
    "anti_patterns", "backup_recovery", "cache_strategy", "cap_theorem",
    "clustering", "index_design", "lock_manager", "mvcc", "newsql",
    "normalization", "nosql", "query_optimization", "replication",
    "sharding", "sql_fundamentals", "transaction",
  ],
  ai: [
    "cnn", "decision_tree", "dimensionality_reduction", "gradient_boosting",
    "linear_regression", "logistic_regression", "ml_fundamentals",
    "neural_network", "nlp", "random_forest", "recommendation", "rnn", "svm",
  ],
}

/**
 * 依赖 numpy 的卡：clustering + 全部 13 张 AI 卡（契约 §14.1）。
 * 缺 numpy 时这些卡读得懂、跑不动。
 */
const NUMPY_CARDS = [
  "clustering", "cnn", "decision_tree", "dimensionality_reduction",
  "gradient_boosting", "linear_regression", "logistic_regression",
  "ml_fundamentals", "neural_network", "nlp", "random_forest",
  "recommendation", "rnn", "svm",
]

/**
 * 这张卡要不要 numpy。
 * @param {string} stem
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function cardNeedsNumpy(stem) {
  return NUMPY_CARDS.indexOf(String(stem === null || stem === undefined ? "" : stem)) !== -1
}
```

在 `module.exports` 里加三行：

```javascript
  GROKKING_CARDS: GROKKING_CARDS,
  NUMPY_CARDS: NUMPY_CARDS,
  cardNeedsNumpy: cardNeedsNumpy,
```

**若 P0 的 `trackOfGrokkingCard(stem)` 用的是另一份写死的清单：** 把它改成查 `GROKKING_CARDS`：

```javascript
function trackOfGrokkingCard(stem) {
  const s = String(stem === null || stem === undefined ? "" : stem)
  if (GROKKING_CARDS.database.indexOf(s) !== -1) return "database"
  if (GROKKING_CARDS.ai.indexOf(s) !== -1) return "ai"
  return null
}
```

**3.3 `core/unit/loader-b.js` —— 缺 numpy 时写进 warnings**

**3.3.1** 在文件头的 require 区加两行：

```javascript
const { cardNeedsNumpy } = require("../track/tracks.js")
const { detectNumpy } = require("../../judge/sandbox.js")
```

（`judge/sandbox.js` 只依赖 node 内置模块，不会形成循环依赖。）

**3.3.2** 在 `parseCardDirectory` 里、`return { unit: unit, warnings: warnings }` **之前**插入：

```javascript
  // 缺 numpy 的卡读得懂、跑不动——把原因写进 warnings，UI 上要显示出来，
  // 不许让用户对着一个 ModuleNotFoundError 发懵。
  const numpyAvailable =
    opts && opts.env && typeof opts.env.numpy === "boolean"
      ? opts.env.numpy
      : detectNumpy(null).available
  if (cardNeedsNumpy(stem) && !numpyAvailable) {
    warnings.push("这张卡需要 numpy，本机没装，暂时跑不了")
  }
```

（`<stem>` 用 loader B 里已有的那个目录名变量；若 P0 的代码里叫别的名字，用它的。）

## Task 7: `server/api-unit-run.js` —— 判题 / 完成 / 问 LLM / 环境自检

**Files:**
- Create: `projects/apps/study-hub/server/api-unit-run.js`
- Modify: `projects/apps/study-hub/server/app.js`（装配新路由 + 把单元索引挂到 `app.units`）
- Create: `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-api-unit-run.test.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `judge/runner.js` 的 `judgeUnit`；`judge/sandbox.js` 的 `detectCompiler` / `detectPython` / `detectNumpy`；`server/router.js` 的 `readJsonBody`；`server/db.js` 的 `store.upsertUnitProgress`；`core/llm/service.js` 的 `llm.explain`
- Produces:
  - `buildUnitRunRoutes(ctx) : Array<{method, pattern, handler}>`
  - `POST /api/units/:id/judge`
  - `POST /api/units/:id/complete`
  - `POST /api/units/:id/ask`
  - `GET /api/env`

**为什么不并进 `server/api-units.js`：** P0/P1 已经建了那个文件并写了 `GET /api/units` 与 `GET /api/units/:id`。P2 **不去改别人的文件**，另起一个 `api-unit-run.js`，在 `server/app.js` 里合并进同一张路由表——照 `api-insights.js` / `api-llm.js` 的既有套路（M5、M6 都是这么干的）。

**判题接口要拿到完整的 Unit（含 `entry` 与 `asserts`）。** `units` 表里只是**物化视图**（`id / track / kind / title / path / status / topics / difficulty / subtracks / updated`），没有 `entry`、没有 `asserts`、没有讲解正文——所以判题接口必须查**内存里的单元索引**（`loadUnits()` 返回的 `byId`），不能查表。

**路由形状（契约 §8.5，Task 1 已冻结）：**

| 方法 | 路径 | 请求 | 响应 |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/units/:id/judge` | `{ code?: string, language?: string }` | `{ verdict, passed, total, durationMs, message, failed, stdout, stderr, unit: {id, kind, title, track} }` |
| POST | `/api/units/:id/complete` | — | `{ unitId, progress, lastAt }` |
| POST | `/api/units/:id/ask` | `{ level?: string }` | `{ text, model }` |
| GET | `/api/env` | — | `{ python: {available, version}, compiler: {available, version}, numpy: {available, version}, notes: string[] }` |

**错误码（冻结）：**
- 三条 `:id` 路由的单元不存在 → 404 `{"error":"单元不存在","id":"<id>"}`
- `judge`：`kind === "problem"` 且 `code` 为空 → 400 `{"error":"代码不能为空"}`；`language` 不在 `["cpp","python"]` → 400 `{"error":"不支持的语言：<lang>","supported":["cpp","python"]}`
- `ask`：LLM 关掉 / 缺密钥 → **仍然 200**，返回中文降级文案（G10）

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

Create `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-api-unit-run.test.js`：

```javascript
"use strict"
/**
 * p2-api-unit-run.test.js —— P2 的单元运行接口
 *
 * 钉住：
 *   1. 四条路由的路径、入参与响应形状（契约 §8.5）；
 *   2. 断言不过时 verdict 是 wrong_answer，不是 500；
 *   3. 断言脚本缺失时 verdict 是 compile_error（附中文说明），不是 500；
 *   4. 单元不存在一律 404「单元不存在」；
 *   5. LLM 走注入的假客户端，全程不联网。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert/strict")
const fs = require("node:fs")
const os = require("node:os")
const path = require("node:path")

const { createApp } = require("../server/app.js")

/** 契约 §3.5 形状的断言脚本（简化版，行为一致） */
const CARD_SCRIPT = [
  "import os, shutil, subprocess, sys, tempfile",
  "DEMO = os.environ.get('STUDY_HUB_DEMO') or os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'main.py')",
  "CHECKS = [('demo 跑起来了', 'OK demo ran')]",
  "def decode(raw):",
  "    for enc in ('utf-8', 'gbk'):",
  "        try:",
  "            return raw.decode(enc)",
  "        except UnicodeDecodeError:",
  "            continue",
  "    return raw.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')",
  "def run_demo():",
  "    if not os.path.exists(DEMO):",
  "        return None, '找不到 demo：' + DEMO",
  "    sandbox = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='study-hub-assert-')",
  "    try:",
  "        target = os.path.join(sandbox, os.path.basename(DEMO))",
  "        shutil.copy2(DEMO, target)",
  "        proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, target], capture_output=True, timeout=180)",
  "        return proc, None",
  "    finally:",
  "        shutil.rmtree(sandbox, ignore_errors=True)",
  "def main():",
  "    proc, err = run_demo()",
  "    if err is not None:",
  "        print(err)",
  "        return 1",
  "    out = decode(proc.stdout)",
  "    print('─── demo 输出开始 ───')",
  "    print(out.rstrip())",
  "    print('─── demo 输出结束 ───')",
  "    failed = []",
  "    for name, needle in CHECKS:",
  "        if needle not in out:",
  "            failed.append(name + '（缺少：' + needle + '）')",
  "    if proc.returncode != 0:",
  "        failed.append('demo 退出码非 0：' + str(proc.returncode))",
  "    if failed:",
  "        for item in failed:",
  "            print('断言失败：' + item)",
  "        print('─── demo 错误输出开始 ───', file=sys.stderr)",
  "        print(decode(proc.stderr).rstrip(), file=sys.stderr)",
  "        print('─── demo 错误输出结束 ───', file=sys.stderr)",
  "        return 1",
  "    print('全部断言通过（%d 条）' % len(CHECKS))",
  "    return 0",
  "if __name__ == '__main__':",
  "    sys.exit(main())",
  "",
].join("\n")

/** 造一个带一张数据库卡 + 一道算法题的 learning 仓 */
function tempLearning() {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "p2-learning-"))

  const cardDir = path.join(root, "scaffold", "grokking", "testcard")
  fs.mkdirSync(cardDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "README.md"), "# 测试卡\n\n这是讲解正文。\n", "utf8")
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "NOTES.md"), "## 笔记\n\n要点一。\n", "utf8")
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cardDir, "main.py"), "print('OK demo ran')\n", "utf8")

  const unitsDir = path.join(root, "units", "database")
  fs.mkdirSync(unitsDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(unitsDir, "testcard.assert.py"), CARD_SCRIPT, "utf8")

  const bankDir = path.join(root, "bank", "interview")
  fs.mkdirSync(bankDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(bankDir, "lc-00007.md"),
    [
      "---",
      "id: lc-00007",
      "source: leetcode",
      "title: 两数之和",
      "difficulty: easy",
      "topics: [哈希表, 数组]",
      "tracks: [interview]",
      "status: todo",
      "needs_fill: false",
      "created: 2026-11-12",
      "updated: 2026-11-12",
      "---",
      "",
      "## 题面",
      "",
      "给定两个整数，求它们的和。",
      "",
      "## 测试用例",
      "",
      "| input | expect |",
      "|---|---|",
      "| 1 2 | 3 |",
      "",
      "## 参考解法",
      "",
      "```cpp",
      "int main() { return 0; }",
      "```",
      "",
    ].join("\n"),
    "utf8"
  )
  return root
}

/** 假客户端：不联网（G12） */
function makeFakeClient() {
  return {
    name: "ark",
    model: "fake-model",
    complete: async function () {
      return "这是一段模型回答。"
    },
  }
}

async function withApp(fn) {
  const app = createApp({
    dbPath: ":memory:",
    learningRoot: tempLearning(),
    // 不加载工程仓自带的题库：测试只看临时 learning 仓里的两个单元
    localBank: null,
    llmClient: makeFakeClient(),
    env: {},
  })
  const port = await app.listen(0)
  try {
    await fn("http://127.0.0.1:" + port, app)
  } finally {
    await app.close()
  }
}

async function postJson(base, urlPath, body) {
  const res = await fetch(base + urlPath, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body || {}),
  })
  return { status: res.status, body: await res.json() }
}

test("POST /api/units/:id/judge 卡片通过时 verdict 是 pass", async function () {
  await withApp(async function (base) {
    const r = await postJson(base, "/api/units/db:testcard/judge", {})
    assert.equal(r.status, 200)
    assert.equal(r.body.verdict, "pass", r.body.message)
    assert.equal(r.body.passed, 2)
    assert.equal(r.body.total, 2)
    assert.equal(r.body.failed, null)
    assert.equal(r.body.unit.id, "db:testcard")
    assert.equal(r.body.unit.kind, "card")
    assert.equal(r.body.unit.track, "database")
    assert.ok(r.body.stdout.indexOf("全部断言通过") !== -1, r.body.stdout)
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(r.body).sort(), [
      "durationMs", "failed", "message", "passed", "stderr",
      "total", "unit", "verdict", "stdout",
    ].sort())
  })
})

test("POST /api/units/:id/judge 断言不过时是 wrong_answer，不是 500", async function () {
  await withApp(async function (base, app) {
    // 把脚本的断言改坏
    const scriptPath = path.join(app.learning.root, "units", "database", "testcard.assert.py")
    fs.writeFileSync(
      scriptPath,
      CARD_SCRIPT.replace("OK demo ran", "绝对不会出现的字符串"),
      "utf8"
    )
    const r = await postJson(base, "/api/units/db:testcard/judge", {})
    assert.equal(r.status, 200)
    assert.equal(r.body.verdict, "wrong_answer", r.body.message)
    assert.ok(r.body.failed, "失败时必须给出 failed")
    assert.equal(r.body.failed.type, "exit")
  })
})

test("POST /api/units/:id/judge 断言脚本缺失时是 compile_error（附中文说明），不是 500", async function () {
  await withApp(async function (base, app) {
    fs.rmSync(path.join(app.learning.root, "units", "database", "testcard.assert.py"))
    const r = await postJson(base, "/api/units/db:testcard/judge", {})
    assert.equal(r.status, 200)
    assert.equal(r.body.verdict, "compile_error", r.body.message)
    assert.ok(r.body.message.indexOf("断言脚本不存在") !== -1, r.body.message)
  })
})

- [ ] **Step 2: 实现 `server/api-unit-run.js`**

Create `projects/apps/study-hub/server/api-unit-run.js`：

```javascript
"use strict"
/**
 * api-unit-run.js —— P2 的单元运行接口
 *
 * 单独一个文件，**不动 P0 的 server/api-units.js**（那是 P0/P1 的地盘）：
 *   POST /api/units/:id/judge     跑断言 -> verdict
 *   POST /api/units/:id/complete  把单元进度置为 done
 *   POST /api/units/:id/ask       问 LLM（单元上下文）
 *   GET  /api/env                 环境自检（python / g++ / numpy）
 *
 * 判题要拿完整的 Unit（含 entry 与 asserts），所以查的是**内存里的单元索引**
 * （loadUnits 的 byId），不是 units 表——那张表只是摘要（契约 §4.2）。
 */

const { readJsonBody, HttpError } = require("./router.js")
const { judgeUnit } = require("../judge/runner.js")
const { detectCompiler, detectPython, detectNumpy } = require("../judge/sandbox.js")

/** 只支持这两种语言（与 judge/runner.js 的 LANGUAGES 一致） */
const SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES = ["cpp", "python"]

/**
 * 服务层用「带 status 的普通 Error」表达业务错误（429 配额用完 / 503 没装配），
 * 路由层只认 HttpError。这里转一道——不然它们统统会变成 500，
 * 前端就分不清「参数写错了」和「服务端炸了」。
 */
function toHttpError(err) {
  if (err instanceof HttpError) return err
  const status = Number(err && err.status)
  if (status >= 400 && status <= 599) {
    return new HttpError(status, String((err && err.message) || "请求失败"))
  }
  return err
}

/**
 * 从单元索引里按 id 取单元。
 * byId 既可能是普通对象（契约 §3.4：{ [id]: Unit }），也可能是 Map——两种都认。
 * @param {{units?: object}} ctx
 * @param {string} id
 * @returns {object|null}
 */
function pickUnit(ctx, id) {
  const src = ctx.units || {}
  const byId = src.byId || src
  if (!byId) return null
  if (typeof byId.get === "function") return byId.get(id) || null
  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, id)) return byId[id]
  return null
}

/** 响应里的单元摘要 */
function unitBrief(unit) {
  return {
    id: String(unit.id || ""),
    kind: String(unit.kind || ""),
    title: String(unit.title || ""),
    track: String(unit.track || ""),
  }
}

/**
 * 组装 P2 的单元运行路由表。
 * @param {{store: object, units: object, llm?: object, learning?: object, sendJson: Function}} ctx
 * @returns {Array<{method: string, pattern: string, handler: Function}>}
 */
function buildUnitRunRoutes(ctx) {
  const routes = []
  const store = ctx.store
  const llm = ctx.llm
  const learningRoot = ctx.learning && ctx.learning.root ? ctx.learning.root : null

  function notFound(res, id) {
    ctx.sendJson(res, 404, { error: "单元不存在", id: String(id) })
  }

  // 判题：一条路径，problem 走用户代码、card 走单元自带断言脚本
  routes.push({
    method: "POST",
    pattern: "/api/units/:id/judge",
    handler: async function (req, res, params) {
      const unit = pickUnit(ctx, params.id)
      if (!unit) {
        notFound(res, params.id)
        return
      }
      const body = await readJsonBody(req)
      const kind = String(unit.kind || "")
      let code = ""
      let language = ""

      // 卡类不收代码：它的入口是单元自带的断言脚本
      if (kind === "problem") {
        code = body.code === null || body.code === undefined ? "" : String(body.code)
        if (code.trim() === "") {
          ctx.sendJson(res, 400, { error: "代码不能为空" })
          return
        }
        const entry = unit.entry || {}
        language = String(body.language || entry.language || "cpp").toLowerCase()
        if (SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES.indexOf(language) === -1) {
          ctx.sendJson(res, 400, {
            error: "不支持的语言：" + language,
            supported: SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES.slice(),
          })
          return
        }
      }

      const result = await judgeUnit({
        unit: unit,
        code: code,
        language: language,
        learningRoot: learningRoot,
      })

      ctx.sendJson(res, 200, {
        verdict: result.verdict,
        passed: result.passedCases,
        total: result.totalCases,
        durationMs: result.durationMs,
        message: result.message,
        failed: result.failedCase || null,
        stdout: result.stdout || "",
        stderr: result.stderr || "",
        unit: unitBrief(unit),
      })
    },
  })

  // 完成：写 unit_progress（复习卡与掌握度是 P1/P3 的事，这里不碰）
  routes.push({
    method: "POST",
    pattern: "/api/units/:id/complete",
    handler: async function (req, res, params) {
      const unit = pickUnit(ctx, params.id)
      if (!unit) {
        notFound(res, params.id)
        return
      }
      await readJsonBody(req)
      if (!store || typeof store.upsertUnitProgress !== "function") {
        ctx.sendJson(res, 503, { error: "进度存储还没装配" })
        return
      }
      const lastAt = new Date().toISOString()
      store.upsertUnitProgress(unit.id, "done", lastAt)
      ctx.sendJson(res, 200, { unitId: unit.id, progress: "done", lastAt: lastAt })
    },
  })

  // 问 LLM：单元上下文（题面或讲解正文）喂给 explain
  routes.push({
    method: "POST",
    pattern: "/api/units/:id/ask",
    handler: async function (req, res, params) {
      const unit = pickUnit(ctx, params.id)
      if (!unit) {
        notFound(res, params.id)
        return
      }
      const body = await readJsonBody(req)
      if (!llm || typeof llm.explain !== "function") {
        // LLM 没装配不是错误：单元页照常能读能判，只是问不了（G10）
        ctx.sendJson(res, 200, {
          text: "LLM 没装配，问不了。可以先自己读讲解、跑一遍断言。",
          model: "null",
        })
        return
      }
      try {
        const result = await llm.explain({
          problem: {
            id: unit.id,
            title: unit.title || "",
            difficulty: unit.difficulty || "medium",
            topics: Array.isArray(unit.topics) ? unit.topics : [],
            statement: unit.statement || "",
          },
          level: String(body.level || "思路"),
          sessionId: body.sessionId,
        })
        ctx.sendJson(res, 200, { text: result.text, model: result.model })
      } catch (err) {
        throw toHttpError(err)
      }
    },
  })

  // 环境自检：只读，不写任何文件
  routes.push({
    method: "GET",
    pattern: "/api/env",
    handler: async function (req, res) {
      const python = detectPython(null)
      const compiler = detectCompiler()
      const numpy = detectNumpy(null)
      const notes = []
      if (!python) {
        notes.push("本机没有找到 Python 解释器，数据库轨与 AI 轨的 demo 跑不了")
      }
      if (!compiler) {
        notes.push("本机没有找到 g++，算法轨的 C++ 代码判不了")
      }
      if (!numpy.available) {
        notes.push("本机没有装 numpy，13 张 AI 卡与 clustering 卡暂时跑不了（装上 numpy 即可运行）")
      }
      if (notes.length === 0) notes.push("运行环境正常")
      ctx.sendJson(res, 200, {
        python: { available: !!python, version: python ? python.version : "" },
        compiler: { available: !!compiler, version: compiler ? compiler.version : "" },
        numpy: { available: !!numpy.available, version: numpy.version || "" },
        notes: notes,
      })
    },
  })

  return routes
}

module.exports = {
  buildUnitRunRoutes: buildUnitRunRoutes,
  SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES: SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES,
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 接线 `server/app.js`**

`server/app.js` 要改三处，**都是加法**（P0/P1 写的代码一行不动）。

**3.1** 在 `function createApp(opts) {` **之前**插入一个模块级 helper：

```javascript
/**
 * 装载三轨共用的单元索引。
 *
 * 契约 §3.4：loadUnits(opts) -> { units, warnings, byId }。
 * 完整 Unit（含 entry / asserts / statement）只活在内存里——
 * units 表只是摘要（契约 §4.2），判题接口必须查这份索引。
 *
 * 扫盘失败不抛异常：单元列表空着，总比服务起不来强。
 * @param {{root: string|null, ok: boolean}} learning
 * @param {object} env
 * @param {object} options
 * @returns {{units: object[], warnings: string[], byId: object}}
 */
function loadUnitsIndex(learning, env, options) {
  if (!learning || !learning.ok) {
    return { units: [], warnings: ["还没连上 learning 仓，单元列表是空的"], byId: {} }
  }
  try {
    const { loadUnits } = require("../core/unit/index.js")
    const { localBankDir } = require("../core/paths.js")
    return loadUnits({
      learningRoot: learning.root,
      localBank: options.localBank === null ? null : localBankDir(env, options.localBank),
      unitsDir: path.join(learning.root, "units"),
      grokkingDir: path.join(learning.root, "scaffold", "grokking"),
    })
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      units: [],
      warnings: ["单元索引装载失败：" + String((err && err.message) || err)],
      byId: {},
    }
  }
}
```

**3.2** 在 `createApp` 里，紧跟着这一行之后（`const routes = require("./api.js").buildRoutes({...})` 整块之后、`// M5：诊断接口` 注释之前）插入：

```javascript
  // P2：三轨共用的单元索引（含 entry / asserts / statement）。
  // P0 的 refreshUnitIndex 只把**摘要**灌进 units 表，完整 Unit 只在内存里，
  // 判题接口要的就是那份。options.units 可注入（测试用）。
  const units = options.units || loadUnitsIndex(learning, env, options)
```

**3.3** 在 `// M6：LLM 接口。` 那段 `routes.push.apply(...)` 之后、`const server = createServer({ routes: routes })` 之前插入：

  // P2：单元运行接口（判题 / 完成 / 问 LLM / 环境自检）。

  routes.push.apply(
    routes,

```

```

## Task 8: `web/pages/unit.js` —— 单元学习页（左读右做）

**Files:**
- Create: `projects/apps/study-hub/web/pages/unit.js`
- Create: `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-unit-page.test.js`
- Modify: `projects/apps/study-hub/web/style.css`（**只追加**两栏布局与代码框样式，不动 `:root`）
- Modify: `projects/apps/study-hub/web/llm.js`（id 正则扩到 `algo:` / `db:` / `ai:`）
- Modify: `projects/apps/study-hub/web/index.html`（加一行 `<script src="/pages/unit.js">`）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - 契约 §8.1：`GET /api/units/:id` → `{ unit: {…Unit 全字段…}, statement_html: string }`；404 → `{error:"单元不存在", id}`
  - 契约 §8.5：`POST /api/units/:id/judge` → `{ verdict, passed, total, durationMs, message, failed, stdout, stderr, unit }`
  - 契约 §8.5：`POST /api/units/:id/complete` → `{ unitId, progress, lastAt }`
  - 契约 §8.5：`POST /api/units/:id/ask` → `{ text, model }`（LLM 关掉时也是 200 + 中文降级文案）
  - 契约 §3.5：demo 回显的两行定界 `─── demo 输出开始 ───` / `─── demo 输出结束 ───`
  - 契约 §9.4：DOM 桩**没有** `innerHTML` / `querySelector` → 可测逻辑必须抽成纯函数
  - `web/ui.js` 的 `pageHead` / `hero` / `panel` / `block` / `statGrid` / `pill` / `kv` / `empty`
- Produces:
  - `web/pages/unit.js` 导出 `buildUnitView(unit, statementHtml)` / `renderUnit(view, unit, statementHtml)`
    / `parseHtmlNodes(html)` / `decodeEntities(text)` / `splitDemoOutput(stdout)`
  - 页面注册 `pages.unit = { title: "单元", render: renderPage }`（契约 §9.1）

> **全局对象的名字**：P1 已把 `window.AlgoPlan` 改成 `window.StudyHub`。
> 下面的代码用 `const A = window.StudyHub || window.AlgoPlan` 兼容两种写法——
> **改的时候按 P1 落地后的实际名字写，两种都能跑**。

> **为什么不用 `innerHTML`**：契约 §9.4 明写 DOM 桩没有 `innerHTML`。
> 所以题面走「HTML → 节点描述树 → 逐节点拼 DOM」三步：
> `parseHtmlNodes`（纯函数，可单测）→ `renderNodes`（只做 DOM 装配）。
> 这样「题面渲染」也能被单测盖住，而不是只能靠肉眼看页面。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

Create `projects/apps/study-hub/tests/p2-unit-page.test.js`：

```javascript
"use strict"
/**
 * p2-unit-page.test.js —— 单元学习页（#/unit/:id）的纯函数与层次闸门
 *
 * 这个页面的可测逻辑都抽成了纯函数（契约 §9.4）：
 *   buildUnitView / parseHtmlNodes / decodeEntities / splitDemoOutput
 * 渲染层用 tests/helpers/dom-stub.js 驱动，验证「左读右做」的层次。
 */

const test = require("node:test")
const assert = require("node:assert")

const stub = require("./helpers/dom-stub.js")
const dom = stub.createDocument()
global.document = dom.document
global.window = global

require("../web/app.js")
const unitPage = require("../web/pages/unit.js")

/** 造一张卡类单元 */
function cardUnit(over) {
  return Object.assign(
    {
      id: "db:mvcc",
      track: "database",
      kind: "card",
      title: "MVCC 与多版本并发控制",
      status: "verified",
      difficulty: "进阶",
      topics: ["事务", "隔离级别"],
      subtracks: ["mvcc"],
      entry: {
        demo: "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py",
        reference: null,
        graded: "script",
        script: "units/database/mvcc.assert.py",
        language: "python",
      },
      asserts: [{ type: "exit", expect: 0 }],
      path: "units/database/mvcc.md",
      statement: "",
    },
    over || {}
  )
}

/** 造一道算法题 */
function problemUnit(over) {
  return Object.assign(
    {
      id: "algo:lc-00007",
      track: "algorithm",
      kind: "problem",
      title: "两数之和",
      status: "verified",
      difficulty: "简单",
      topics: ["数组"],
      subtracks: ["fundamentals"],
      entry: { demo: null, reference: null, graded: "user", script: null, language: "python" },
      asserts: [{ type: "output", input: "1 2", expect: "3", cmp: "exact" }],
      path: "bank/algorithm/lc-00007.md",
      statement: "",
    },
    over || {}
  )
}

/** 在树里找出所有 class 精确匹配的节点 */
function findAllByClass(node, className) {
  const out = []
  function walk(n) {
    if (!n) return
    if (n.className === className) out.push(n)
    ;(n.children || []).forEach(walk)
  }
  walk(node)
  return out
}

/** 在树里找出第一个 class 精确匹配的节点 */
function findByClass(node, className) {
  return findAllByClass(node, className)[0] || null
}

/** 把树里所有文本拼起来 */
function collectText(node) {
  let out = ""
  function walk(n) {
    if (!n) return
    if (n.textContent) out += " " + n.textContent
    ;(n.children || []).forEach(walk)
  }
  walk(node)
  return out
}

/** 节点描述树的形状（只看元素标签） */
function shape(nodes) {
  const out = []
  ;(nodes || []).forEach(function (n) {
    if (!n || n.tag === "#text") return
    out.push(n.tag + "(" + shape(n.children) + ")")
  })
  return out.join("")
}

/** 节点描述树里的全部文字 */
function textOf(nodes) {
  let out = ""
  ;(nodes || []).forEach(function (n) {
    if (!n) return
    if (n.tag === "#text") {
      out += n.text
      return
    }
    out += textOf(n.children)
  })
  return out
}

/* ---------------- 纯函数 ---------------- */

test("buildUnitView：卡类单元给出中文的轨道 / 形态 / 状态", function () {
  const v = unitPage.buildUnitView(cardUnit(), "<p>正文</p>")
  assert.strictEqual(v.id, "db:mvcc")
  assert.strictEqual(v.trackLabel, "数据库")
  assert.strictEqual(v.kindLabel, "知识卡")
  assert.strictEqual(v.statusLabel, "已验证")
  assert.strictEqual(v.isProblem, false)
  assert.strictEqual(v.assertCount, 1)
  assert.strictEqual(v.demoPath, "scaffold/grokking/mvcc/main.py")
  assert.strictEqual(v.scriptPath, "units/database/mvcc.assert.py")
})

test("buildUnitView：算法题与知识卡分得开", function () {
  const v = unitPage.buildUnitView(problemUnit(), "<p>题面</p>")
  assert.strictEqual(v.kindLabel, "算法题")
  assert.strictEqual(v.isProblem, true)
  assert.strictEqual(v.graded, "user")
  assert.strictEqual(v.trackLabel, "算法")
})

test("buildUnitView：题面为空时说清没有正文", function () {
  const v = unitPage.buildUnitView(cardUnit(), "")
  assert.strictEqual(v.hasStatement, false)
  assert.strictEqual(v.statementNodes.length, 0)
})

test("parseHtmlNodes：标题、段落、列表都认得", function () {
  const html = "<h2>版本链</h2><p>每行数据有多个版本。</p><ul><li>写操作新增版本</li><li>读操作看快照</li></ul>"
  const nodes = unitPage.parseHtmlNodes(html)
  assert.strictEqual(shape(nodes), "h2()p()ul(li()li())")
  assert.strictEqual(textOf(nodes), "版本链每行数据有多个版本。写操作新增版本读操作看快照")
})

test("parseHtmlNodes：行内标签与有序列表都认得", function () {
  const html = "<p>看 <strong>版本链</strong> 与 <code>ReadView</code></p><ol><li>第一步</li></ol>"
  const nodes = unitPage.parseHtmlNodes(html)
  assert.strictEqual(shape(nodes), "p(strong()code())ol(li())")
  assert.ok(textOf(nodes).indexOf("版本链") !== -1)
})

test("parseHtmlNodes：HTML 实体解得开", function () {
  const nodes = unitPage.parseHtmlNodes("<p>a &amp; b &lt;c&gt; &#65;</p>")
  assert.strictEqual(textOf(nodes), "a & b <c> A")
})

test("parseHtmlNodes：认不出的标签也不丢内容", function () {
  const nodes = unitPage.parseHtmlNodes("<p>hello <blink>world</blink></p>")
  assert.strictEqual(shape(nodes), "p(blink())")
  assert.strictEqual(textOf(nodes), "hello world")
})

test("splitDemoOutput：按契约 §3.5 的定界行切出 demo 输出", function () {
  const stdout =
    "─── demo 输出开始 ───\nhello demo\n─── demo 输出结束 ───\n全部断言通过（1 条）\n"
  const split = unitPage.splitDemoOutput(stdout)
  assert.strictEqual(split.hasMarkers, true)
  assert.strictEqual(split.demoOutput, "hello demo")
  assert.ok(split.rest.indexOf("全部断言通过") !== -1)
  assert.strictEqual(split.rest.indexOf("demo 输出开始"), -1)
})

test("splitDemoOutput：没有定界行就原样返回，不炸", function () {
  const split = unitPage.splitDemoOutput("全部断言通过（1 条）")
  assert.strictEqual(split.hasMarkers, false)
  assert.strictEqual(split.demoOutput, "")
  assert.strictEqual(split.rest, "全部断言通过（1 条）")
})

/* ---------------- 渲染层 ---------------- */

test("renderUnit：左读右做两栏，一个页头一个主卡", function () {
  const unit = cardUnit()
  const html = "<h2>版本链</h2><p>每行数据有多个版本。</p>"
  const view = unitPage.buildUnitView(unit, html)
  const root = unitPage.renderUnit(view, unit, html)

  assert.strictEqual(findAllByClass(root, "page-head").length, 1, "单元页有且只有一个页头")
  assert.strictEqual(findAllByClass(root, "panel hero").length, 1, "主行动有且只有一个主卡")
  assert.ok(findByClass(root, "unit-col-read"), "左栏（读）要在")
  assert.ok(findByClass(root, "unit-col-do"), "右栏（做）要在")
  assert.ok(findByClass(root, "stat-grid"), "数字要摆成数字块")
  assert.ok(findByClass(root, "panel quiet"), "元信息是背景信息，用静默卡")
})

test("renderUnit：题面正文进了 DOM（全程没用 innerHTML）", function () {
  const unit = cardUnit()
  const html = "<h2>版本链</h2><p>每行数据有多个版本。</p>"
  const view = unitPage.buildUnitView(unit, html)
  const root = unitPage.renderUnit(view, unit, html)
  const text = collectText(root)
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("版本链") !== -1, "题面标题要渲染出来")
  assert.ok(text.indexOf("每行数据有多个版本") !== -1, "题面正文要渲染出来")
})

test("renderUnit：卡片形态右栏有「运行断言」按钮", function () {
  const unit = cardUnit()
  const view = unitPage.buildUnitView(unit, "<p>正文</p>")
  const root = unitPage.renderUnit(view, unit, "<p>正文</p>")
  assert.ok(stub.findById(root, "unit-run"), "卡片要有运行断言的按钮")
  assert.ok(stub.findById(root, "unit-complete"), "要有完成本单元的按钮")
  assert.strictEqual(stub.findById(root, "unit-code"), null, "卡片不该有代码编辑区")
})

test("renderUnit：算法题形态右栏有代码编辑区与「提交判题」按钮", function () {
  const unit = problemUnit()
  const view = unitPage.buildUnitView(unit, "<p>题面</p>")
  const root = unitPage.renderUnit(view, unit, "<p>题面</p>")

test("renderUnit：没正文时给出中文空态，不白屏", function () {

  const view = unitPage.buildUnitView(unit, "")
  const root = unitPage.renderUnit(view, unit, "")
  assert.ok(findByClass(root, "empty"), "没正文要给空态")
  assert.ok(collectText(root).indexOf("还没有正文") !== -1)

/* ---------------- web/llm.js 的 id 正则（契约 §9.2） ---------------- */

test("llm.js 的 id 正则认得三种新形态，也还认旧题号", function () {
  const llm = require("../web/llm.js")
  const saved = global.location
  function idOf(hash) {
    global.location = { hash: hash }
    try {
      return llm.findProblemIdInHash()
    } finally {
      global.location = saved
    }
  }
  assert.strictEqual(idOf("#/unit/algo:lc-00007"), "algo:lc-00007")
  assert.strictEqual(idOf("#/unit/db:mvcc"), "db:mvcc")
  assert.strictEqual(idOf("#/unit/ai:cnn"), "ai:cnn")
  assert.strictEqual(idOf("#/unit/db%3Amvcc"), "db:mvcc", "百分号编码的冒号也要认")
  assert.strictEqual(idOf("#/unit/lc-00001"), "lc-00001", "旧形态不能改")
})

- [ ] **Step 3: 写 `web/pages/unit.js`**

Create `projects/apps/study-hub/web/pages/unit.js`：

```javascript
"use strict"
/**
 * unit.js —— 单元学习页：左栏读、右栏做
 *
 * 一个单元有两种形态（契约 §1）：
 *   kind: "card"    知识卡 —— 左栏讲解，右栏跑 demo、看断言结果
 *   kind: "problem" 算法题 —— 左栏题面，右栏代码编辑区 + 判题结果
 *
 * 页面只读单元；唯一的写动作是「完成本单元」（POST /api/units/:id/complete）。
 *
 * 题面是服务端给的 statement_html（契约 §8.1）。
 * DOM 桩没有 innerHTML（契约 §9.4），所以**不用 innerHTML**：
 * 先把 HTML 解析成节点描述树（parseHtmlNodes，纯函数），再逐节点拼 DOM。
 *
 * 分工：
 *   纯函数（buildUnitView / parseHtmlNodes / decodeEntities / splitDemoOutput）——可单测
 *   渲染层（renderUnit / renderPage）——只做 DOM 装配
 */

(function () {
  /** 全局对象：P1 起是 window.StudyHub；过渡期兼容 window.AlgoPlan */
  const A =
    (typeof window !== "undefined" && (window.StudyHub || window.AlgoPlan)) ||
    (typeof globalThis !== "undefined" && (globalThis.StudyHub || globalThis.AlgoPlan)) ||
    {}

  const el = A.el || (typeof require === "function" ? require("../ui.js").el : null)

  /** 公共界面构件。浏览器里 ui.js 先加载；Node 下 require 同一份文件——实现只有一份。 */
  const UI =
    (typeof window !== "undefined" &&
      (window.AlgoPlanUI || (window.StudyHub && window.StudyHub.ui) || (window.AlgoPlan && window.AlgoPlan.ui))) ||
    (typeof require === "function" ? require("../ui.js") : null)

  /** 轨道 → 中文名 */
  const TRACK_LABEL = { algorithm: "算法", database: "数据库", ai: "AI" }

  /** 单元状态 → 中文名（契约 §1.4 的四个取值） */
  const STATUS_LABEL = { todo: "待学", draft: "草稿", verified: "已验证", rejected: "已驳回" }

  /** 单元状态 → 药丸色调 */
  const STATUS_TONE = { todo: "", draft: "warn", verified: "ok", rejected: "bad" }

  /** verdict → 中文说法：颜色之外再来一份文字，不靠颜色单独表意 */
  const VERDICT_TEXT = {
    pass: "通过",
    wrong_answer: "答案错误",
    timeout: "超时",
    compile_error: "编译失败",
    runtime_error: "运行时错误",
  }

  /** verdict → 药丸色调 */
  const VERDICT_TONE = {
    pass: "ok",
    wrong_answer: "bad",
    timeout: "warn",
    compile_error: "bad",
    runtime_error: "bad",
  }

  /** demo 回显的定界行（契约 §3.5 冻结，不许改） */
  const DEMO_OUT_START = "─── demo 输出开始 ───"
  const DEMO_OUT_END = "─── demo 输出结束 ───"

  /** 自闭合标签：没有结束标签 */
  const VOID_TAGS = {
    br: true, hr: true, img: true, input: true, meta: true, link: true, col: true, source: true,
  }

  /** HTML 实体表（只收我们自己那套 markdown 转换器会吐出来的那些） */
  const ENTITIES = {
    amp: "&", lt: "<", gt: ">", quot: '"', apos: "'", nbsp: " ", "#39": "'", "#x27": "'",
  }

  /** 页面内状态：当前单元与上一次结果跨渲染保留（切页回来不丢） */
  const pageState = {
    unit: null,
    statementHtml: "",
    code: "",
    language: "python",
    result: null,
    resultBox: null,
    llmText: "",
    doneText: "",
  }

  /**
   * 解 HTML 实体。
   * @param {string} text
   * @returns {string}
   */
  function decodeEntities(text) {
    return String(text === null || text === undefined ? "" : text).replace(
      /&(#?[a-zA-Z0-9]+);/g,
      function (all, name) {
        if (name.charAt(0) === "#") {
          const hex = name.charAt(1) === "x" || name.charAt(1) === "X"
          const code = hex ? parseInt(name.slice(2), 16) : parseInt(name.slice(1), 10)
          if (Number.isFinite(code) && code > 0 && code <= 0x10ffff) {
            try {
              return String.fromCodePoint(code)
            } catch (e) {
              return all
            }
          }
          return all
        }
        const key = name.toLowerCase()
        return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ENTITIES, key) ? ENTITIES[key] : all
      }
    )
  }

  /**
   * 解析标签属性。
   * @param {string} raw
   * @returns {Object<string,string>}
   */
  function parseAttrs(raw) {
    const attrs = {}
    const re = /([a-zA-Z_:][-a-zA-Z0-9_:.]*)\s*=\s*("([^"]*)"|'([^']*)'|([^\s"'>]+))/g
    let m
    while ((m = re.exec(String(raw || ""))) !== null) {
      let value = ""
      if (m[3] !== undefined) value = m[3]
      else if (m[4] !== undefined) value = m[4]
      else if (m[5] !== undefined) value = m[5]
      attrs[m[1].toLowerCase()] = decodeEntities(value)
    }
    return attrs
  }

  /**
   * 把一小段 HTML 解析成节点描述树。
   * 只认我们自己那套 markdown 转换器会吐出来的标签；认不出的标签照样成节点，
   * **内容一个字都不丢**（契约 §8.1：未知语法原样输出）。
   * @param {string} html
   * @returns {Array<{tag: string, attrs: object, children: Array}>}
   */
  function parseHtmlNodes(html) {
    const text = String(html === null || html === undefined ? "" : html)
    const root = { tag: "#root", attrs: {}, children: [] }
    const stack = [root]
    const re = /<!--[\s\S]*?-->|<\/?([a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9]*)((?:\s[^<>]*?)?)\/?>/g
    let last = 0
    let m

    function pushText(parent, raw) {
      const value = decodeEntities(raw)
      if (value === "") return
      parent.children.push({ tag: "#text", attrs: {}, children: [], text: value })
    }

    while ((m = re.exec(text)) !== null) {
      const chunk = text.slice(last, m.index)
      if (chunk) pushText(stack[stack.length - 1], chunk)
      last = re.lastIndex
      const raw = m[0]
      if (raw.slice(0, 4) === "<!--") continue
      const closing = raw.charAt(1) === "/"
      const tag = String(m[1] || "").toLowerCase()
      if (closing) {
        for (let i = stack.length - 1; i > 0; i -= 1) {
          if (stack[i].tag === tag) {
            stack.length = i
            break
          }
        }
        continue
      }
      const node = { tag: tag, attrs: parseAttrs(m[2] || ""), children: [] }
      stack[stack.length - 1].children.push(node)
      if (!VOID_TAGS[tag]) stack.push(node)
    }

    const tail = text.slice(last)
    if (tail) pushText(stack[stack.length - 1], tail)
    return root.children
  }

  /**
   * 把节点描述树拼成 DOM 节点数组。
   * @param {Array} nodes
   * @returns {Array}
   */
  function renderNodes(nodes) {
    const out = []
    ;(nodes || []).forEach(function (node) {
      if (!node) return
      if (node.tag === "#text") {
        out.push(document.createTextNode(node.text || ""))
        return
      }
      const attrs = {}
      Object.keys(node.attrs || {}).forEach(function (key) {
        attrs[key] = node.attrs[key]
      })
      out.push(el(node.tag, attrs, renderNodes(node.children)))
    })
    return out
  }

  /**
   * 从脚本输出里把 demo 的回显切出来（契约 §3.5 的两行定界）。
   * 切不出来就原样返回——老脚本没有回显也不该因此炸掉。
   * @param {string} stdout
   * @returns {{demoOutput: string, rest: string, hasMarkers: boolean}}
   */
  function splitDemoOutput(stdout) {
    const text = String(stdout === null || stdout === undefined ? "" : stdout)
    const i = text.indexOf(DEMO_OUT_START)
    const j = text.indexOf(DEMO_OUT_END)
    if (i === -1 || j === -1 || j < i) {
      return { demoOutput: "", rest: text, hasMarkers: false }
    }
    const demoOutput = text
      .slice(i + DEMO_OUT_START.length, j)
      .replace(/^\r?\n/, "")
      .replace(/\s+$/, "")
    const rest = (text.slice(0, i) + text.slice(j + DEMO_OUT_END.length)).trim()
    return { demoOutput: demoOutput, rest: rest, hasMarkers: true }
  }

  /**
   * 把单元整理成页面要用的视图数据（纯函数）。
   * @param {object} unit
   * @param {string} statementHtml
   * @returns {object}
   */
  function buildUnitView(unit, statementHtml) {
    const u = unit || {}
    const entry = u.entry || {}
    const track = String(u.track || "")
    const kind = String(u.kind || "")
    const isProblem = kind === "problem"
    const status = String(u.status || "todo")
    const html = String(statementHtml === null || statementHtml === undefined ? "" : statementHtml)
    return {
      id: String(u.id || ""),
      title: String(u.title || "未命名单元"),
      track: track,
      trackLabel: TRACK_LABEL[track] || "未知轨道",
      kind: kind,
      kindLabel: isProblem ? "算法题" : "知识卡",
      isProblem: isProblem,
      graded: String(entry.graded || (isProblem ? "user" : "script")),
      status: status,
      statusLabel: STATUS_LABEL[status] || status,
      statusTone: STATUS_TONE[status] || "",
      difficulty: String(u.difficulty || ""),
      topics: Array.isArray(u.topics) ? u.topics.map(String) : [],
      subtracks: Array.isArray(u.subtracks) ? u.subtracks.map(String) : [],
      demoPath: String(entry.demo || ""),
      scriptPath: String(entry.script || ""),
      language: String(entry.language || (isProblem ? "cpp" : "python")),
      assertCount: Array.isArray(u.asserts) ? u.asserts.length : 0,
      hasStatement: html.trim() !== "",
      statementNodes: parseHtmlNodes(html),
    }
  }

  /**
   * 画结果区：verdict 徽章 + 中文说明 + demo 输出 + 没过的断言。
   * @param {HTMLElement} box
   * @param {object} [result] 判题结果；不给就画 pageState.result
   */
  function paintResult(box, result) {
    if (!box) return
    box.textContent = ""
    const res = result === undefined ? pageState.result : result

    if (pageState.doneText) {
      box.appendChild(el("p", { class: "muted", text: pageState.doneText }))
    }
    if (pageState.llmText) {
      box.appendChild(el("div", { class: "llm-out", text: pageState.llmText }))
    }
    if (!res) {
      box.appendChild(UI.empty("还没跑过", "点上面的按钮跑一次，这里会显示 verdict 与 demo 的输出。"))
      return
    }

    const tone = VERDICT_TONE[res.verdict] || ""
    box.appendChild(
      el("div", { class: "row" }, [
        UI.pill(VERDICT_TEXT[res.verdict] || String(res.verdict), tone),
        el("span", {
          class: "muted",
          text: String(res.passed) + "/" + String(res.total) + " 条通过 · " + String(res.durationMs) + " 毫秒",
        }),
      ])
    )
    box.appendChild(el("p", { class: "verdict-message " + tone, text: String(res.message || "") }))

    const split = splitDemoOutput(res.stdout)
    if (split.hasMarkers) {
      box.appendChild(
        UI.block({ title: "demo 输出", children: [el("pre", { class: "unit-output", text: split.demoOutput })] })
      )
    }
    if (split.rest) {
      box.appendChild(
        UI.block({ title: "脚本输出", children: [el("pre", { class: "unit-output", text: split.rest })] })
      )
    }
    if (res.failed) {
      box.appendChild(
        UI.block({
          title: "没过的断言",
          children: [
            el("p", {
              class: "muted",
              text:
                "第 " +
                String(Number(res.failed.index) + 1) +
                " 条（" +
                String(res.failed.type) +
                "）：" +
                String(res.failed.reason),
            }),
          ],
        })
      )
    }
    if (res.stderr) {
      box.appendChild(
        UI.block({ title: "错误输出", children: [el("pre", { class: "unit-output", text: String(res.stderr) })] })
      )
    }
  }

  /**
   * 跑一次判题（卡片跑断言脚本，算法题跑用户代码）。
   */
  async function onJudge() {
    const unit = pageState.unit
    if (!unit) return
    const body = {}
    if (pageState.isProblem) {
      body.code = pageState.code
      body.language = pageState.language
    }
    try {
      pageState.result = await A.postJson("/api/units/" + encodeURIComponent(unit.id) + "/judge", body)
    } catch (err) {
      pageState.result = {
        verdict: "runtime_error",
        passed: 0,
        total: 0,
        durationMs: 0,
        message: String((err && err.message) || err),
        failed: null,
        stdout: "",
        stderr: "",
      }
    }
    paintResult(pageState.resultBox)
  }

  /**
   * 问 LLM。LLM 关掉时接口也是 200 + 中文降级文案，这里照样显示。
   */
  async function onAsk() {
    const unit = pageState.unit
    if (!unit) return
    try {
      const res = await A.postJson("/api/units/" + encodeURIComponent(unit.id) + "/ask", {})
      pageState.llmText = String((res && res.text) || "")
    } catch (err) {
      pageState.llmText = "问不了：" + String((err && err.message) || err)
    }
    paintResult(pageState.resultBox)
  }

  /**
   * 标记「完成本单元」。
   */
  async function onComplete() {
    const unit = pageState.unit
    if (!unit) return
    try {
      const res = await A.postJson("/api/units/" + encodeURIComponent(unit.id) + "/complete", {})
      pageState.doneText = "已标记完成（" + String((res && res.lastAt) || "") + "）"
    } catch (err) {
      pageState.doneText = "标记失败：" + String((err && err.message) || err)
    }
    paintResult(pageState.resultBox)
  }

  /**
   * 渲染单元页。
   * @param {object} view buildUnitView 的产物
   * @param {object} unit 原始单元
   * @param {string} [statementHtml] 题面 HTML（给了就重新解析）
   * @returns {HTMLElement}
   */
  function renderUnit(view, unit, statementHtml) {
    const v = view || {}
    const nodes = typeof statementHtml === "string" ? parseHtmlNodes(statementHtml) : (v.statementNodes || [])

    const head = UI.pageHead({
      eyebrow: "单元 · " + String(v.trackLabel || "未知轨道"),
      title: String(v.title || "未命名单元"),
      sub:
        String(v.kindLabel || "单元") +
        " · " +
        String(v.statusLabel || "待学") +
        " · " +
        String(v.assertCount || 0) +
        " 条断言",
    })

    // ── 左栏：读 ──
    const readKids = []
    if (v.hasStatement) {
      readKids.push.apply(readKids, renderNodes(nodes))
    } else {
      readKids.push(
        UI.empty("这个单元还没有正文", "去 learning 仓的单元文件里补一份讲解，页面会自己显示出来。")
      )
    }
    readKids.push(
      el("div", { class: "row" }, [
        el("button", { class: "btn", id: "unit-ask", text: "问 LLM", onclick: onAsk }),
      ])
    )
    const readCol = el("div", { class: "unit-col unit-col-read" }, [
      UI.panel({ title: "讲解", children: readKids }),
      UI.panel({
        title: "元信息",
        className: "quiet",
        children: [
          UI.kv([
            ["单元 ID", String(v.id || "")],
            ["轨道", String(v.trackLabel || "")],
            ["形态", String(v.kindLabel || "")],
            ["状态", String(v.statusLabel || "")],
            ["难度", String(v.difficulty || "未标")],
            ["知识点", (v.topics || []).join("、") || "无"],
            ["子轨", (v.subtracks || []).join("、") || "无"],
            ["demo 路径", String(v.demoPath || "无")],
            ["断言脚本", String(v.scriptPath || "无")],
          ]),
        ],
      }),
    ])

    // ── 右栏：做 ──
    const doKids = []
    doKids.push(
      UI.statGrid([
        { label: "断言条数", value: String(v.assertCount || 0) },
        { label: "知识点", value: String((v.topics || []).length) },
        { label: "状态", value: String(v.statusLabel || "待学"), tone: String(v.statusTone || "") },
      ])
    )

    if (v.isProblem) {
      const editor = el("textarea", { id: "unit-code", class: "code-editor" })
      editor.value = String(pageState.code || "")
      doKids.push(el("div", { class: "unit-code-wrap" }, [editor]))
      const picker = el("select", { id: "unit-language" }, [
        el("option", { value: "cpp", text: "C++" }),
        el("option", { value: "python", text: "Python" }),
      ])
      picker.value = String(pageState.language || "python")
      doKids.push(
        el("div", { class: "row" }, [
          picker,
          el("button", { class: "btn", id: "unit-judge", text: "提交判题", onclick: onJudge }),
        ])
      )
    } else {
      doKids.push(
        el("div", { class: "row" }, [
          el("button", { class: "btn", id: "unit-run", text: "运行断言", onclick: onJudge }),
          el("button", { class: "btn", id: "unit-rerun", text: "重新运行", onclick: onJudge }),
        ])
      )
    }
    doKids.push(
      el("div", { class: "row" }, [
        el("button", { class: "btn", id: "unit-complete", text: "完成本单元", onclick: onComplete }),
      ])
    )

    const resultBox = el("div", { class: "unit-result", id: "unit-result" })
    pageState.resultBox = resultBox
    paintResult(resultBox)
    doKids.push(resultBox)

    const heroSub = v.isProblem
      ? "写完代码点「提交判题」，右栏会显示 verdict 与没过的用例。"
      : "点「运行断言」跑一遍 demo，右栏会显示 verdict、demo 的输出与没过的断言。"

    const doCol = el("div", { class: "unit-col unit-col-do" }, [
      UI.hero({ title: "动手做", sub: heroSub, children: doKids }),
    ])

    return el("div", { class: "unit-page" }, [
      head,
      el("div", { class: "unit-layout" }, [readCol, doCol]),
    ])
  }

  /**
   * 页面入口：拉单元 → 建视图 → 画。
   * @param {HTMLElement} holder
   * @param {{id?: string}} params 来自 hash 路由的 params
   */
  async function renderPage(holder, params) {
    const p = params || {}
    const id = String(p.id || "")
    holder.textContent = ""
    holder.appendChild(el("div", { class: "panel", text: "正在打开单元…" }))

    if (!id) {
      holder.textContent = ""
      holder.appendChild(UI.pageHead({ eyebrow: "单元", title: "单元", sub: "地址里没有单元编号。" }))
      holder.appendChild(UI.empty("地址不完整", "从单元库里点一个单元进来。"))
      return
    }

    let payload = null
    try {
      payload = await A.getJson("/api/units/" + encodeURIComponent(id))
    } catch (err) {
      holder.textContent = ""
      holder.appendChild(
        UI.pageHead({ eyebrow: "单元", title: "单元没打开", sub: "下面是具体原因。" })
      )
      holder.appendChild(
        el("section", { class: "panel" }, [
          el("h2", { text: "加载失败" }),
          el("p", { class: "error", text: String((err && err.message) || err) }),
        ])
      )
      return
    }

    const unit = (payload && payload.unit) || {}
    const html = String((payload && payload.statement_html) || "")
    pageState.unit = unit
    pageState.statementHtml = html
    pageState.isProblem = unit.kind === "problem"
    pageState.view = buildUnitView(unit, html)

    holder.textContent = ""
    holder.appendChild(renderUnit(pageState.view, unit, html))
  }

  if (A && A.pages) {
    A.pages.unit = { title: "单元", render: renderPage }
  }

  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) {
    module.exports = {
      buildUnitView: buildUnitView,
      renderUnit: renderUnit,
      parseHtmlNodes: parseHtmlNodes,
      decodeEntities: decodeEntities,
      splitDemoOutput: splitDemoOutput,
      paintResult: paintResult,
      render: renderPage,
      TRACK_LABEL: TRACK_LABEL,
      STATUS_LABEL: STATUS_LABEL,
      VERDICT_TEXT: VERDICT_TEXT,
      DEMO_OUT_START: DEMO_OUT_START,
      DEMO_OUT_END: DEMO_OUT_END,
    }
  }
})()
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 追加 `web/style.css` 的两栏样式**

在 `web/style.css` **文件末尾**追加（**一个字都不动 `:root`**）：

```css
/* ---- P2：单元页（左读右做两栏） ---- */
.unit-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 16px;
  align-items: start;
}
.unit-col { min-width: 0; }
.unit-col .panel { margin-bottom: 14px; }
.unit-col .panel:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }
.code-editor {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 260px;
  background: var(--bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--fg);
  font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
  font-size: 13px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  resize: vertical;
}
.unit-output {
  margin: 0;
  background: var(--bg);
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  white-space: pre-wrap;
  word-break: break-word;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  max-height: 320px;
  overflow: auto;
}
.unit-result { margin-top: 12px; }
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .unit-layout { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 改 `web/llm.js` 的 id 正则**

`web/llm.js` 的 `findProblemIdInHash()` 现在只认旧题号（`lc-00001` 这类）。
契约 §9.2 明写「P2 起该正则要扩展到新的 id 形态 `algo:lc-00007` / `db:mvcc` / `ai:cnn`」。

把该函数整个替换成：

```javascript
  /** 地址栏里的单元 id：旧题号（lc-00001）与新单元 id（algo:lc-00007 / db:mvcc / ai:cnn） */
  function findProblemIdInHash() {
    var hash = (global.location && global.location.hash) || ""
    // 旧形态：lc-00001。保留原样返回，不改老行为。
    var old = /(?:^|[#\/=])(lc|nc|jz|self|llm)-[0-9a-z]{5,12}/.exec(hash)
    if (old) return old[0]
    // P2 起的新形态：<trackCode>:<stem>
    // 冒号可能原样出现（#/unit/db:mvcc），也可能被编码成 %3A（#/unit/db%3Amvcc）——两种都认
    var neu = /(?:^|[#\/=])(algo|db|ai)(?::|%3A)([a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*)/i.exec(hash)
    if (neu) return neu[1].toLowerCase() + ":" + neu[2]
    return ""
  }
```

> **为什么新形态要单独返回 `neu[1] + ":" + neu[2]`**：正则的前导 `(?:^|[#\/=])` 会被一起匹配出来，
> 直接返回 `m[0]` 会带上那个 `/`。新形态的 id 要能直接当接口参数用，所以只取 id 部分。
>
> **为什么 `:` 与 `%3A` 都要认**：单元库页（Task 9）拼链接时用 `encodeURIComponent(id)`，
> 冒号会被编码成 `%3A`；而契约 §9.2 的示例是原样的 `#/unit/db:mvcc`。
> 两种写法都可能在地址栏里出现，正则只认一种就会「有时能点开、有时点不开」。

    findProblemIdInHash: findProblemIdInHash,
```
